Let me start over with the equations.

Let the original count at box j be O_j.

After taking k balls from box start:
- Box start has final_count[start] = O_start - k
- For any other box j, if j appears in the distribution sequence, then final_count[j] = O_j + (1 if j is in the distribution)

The distribution sequence is: (start+1) % n, (start+2) % n, ..., (start+k) % n

Let D_j be the number of times box j (0-indexed) appears in this sequence.

Then:
- O_start = final[start] + k
- O_j = final[j] - D_j for j != start

We need to find start and k such that:
1. (start + k) % n = x - 1
2. D_j >= 0 for all j
3. O_start >= 0 (which is true since k >= 1 and final[start] >= 0)
4. O_j >= 0 for all j != start, i.e., final[j] >= D_j for all j

For fixed start, k must satisfy (start + k) % n = x - 1, so k = (x-1 - start) % n + t * n for t >= 0.

Try t=0, t=1, etc. until we find one that works.

Since the distribution pattern has period n, and D_j depends on k mod n, we only need to try a few values of t.

In practice, we probably only need to try t=0 and t=1, but to be safe, let's try until we find one.

However, given the constraints, there might be a mathematical insight.

Looking at the distribution of D_j:

For a sequence of length k starting from (start+1) % n to (start+k) % n in circle, the values D_j are the number of times each box is visited.

The sum of D_j over all j is exactly k.

We need final[j] >= D_j for all j != start.

Also, O_start = final[start] + k >= 0.

Since final[j] >= 0, and D_j >= 0, we just need final[j] >= D_j.

For a fixed start, we try k values in arithmetic sequence with difference n.

We can try k0 = (x-1 - start) % n, and if k0 == 0 then k0 = n.

Then try k = k0, k0+n, k0+2n, ...

We need to find the first k for which for all j, final[j] >= D_j where D_j is the frequency in the sequence from (start+1) to (start+k).

Let me implement this:

```python
n, x = map(int, input().split())
final = list(map(int, input().split()))

# Try each possible starting box (0-indexed)
for start in range(n):
    target = x - 1  # 0-indexed target
    
    # k must satisfy (start + k) % n = target
    # smallest k is k0 = (target - start) % n
    k0 = (target - start) % n
    if k0 == 0:
        k0 = n
    
    # Try values of k = k0, k0+n, k0+2n, ...
    k = k0
    while True:
        # Calculate distribution frequencies
        freq = [0] * n
        # Distribution goes to boxes: (start+1) % n, (start+2) % n, ..., (start+k) % n
        for j in range(1, k + 1):
            box = (start + j) % n
            freq[box] += 1
        
        # Check if all frequencies are within final counts
        valid = True
        for j in range(n):
            if j == start:
                # original count = final[j] + k
                if final[j] + k < 0:
                    valid = False
                    break
            else:
                # original count = final[j] - freq[j]
                if final[j] - freq[j] < 0:
                    valid = False
                    break
        
        if valid:
            # Found a valid solution
            original = final[:]
            # For each box, original[j] = final[j] - freq[j] for j != start
            for j in range(n):
                if j != start:
                    original[j] = final[j] - freq[j]
            original[start] = final[start] + k
            print(' '.join(map(str, original)))
            break
            
        k += n
```

I had a mistake in the formula for the source box.

The source box both loses k balls and may receive some back.

So the correct formulas are:
- For any box j: 
  - if j == start: original[j] = final[j] + k - d_j
  - if j != start: original[j] = final[j] - d_j

And we need original[j] >= 0 for all j.

For a fixed start, we need to find k such that:
- (start + k) % n = x - 1
- for all j, if j != start: final[j] - d_j >= 0
- for j = start: final[j] + k - d_j >= 0

With k = k0 + t*n for t >= 0, where k0 = (x-1 - start) % n, and if k0 == 0 then k0 = n.

Let me implement this:

```python
n, x = map(int, input().split())
final = list(map(int, input().split()))

# Try each possible starting box (0-indexed)
for start in range(n):
    target = x - 1  # 0-indexed target
    
    # k must satisfy (start + k) % n = target
    k0 = (target - start) % n
    if k0 == 0:
        k0 = n
    
    k = k0
    while True:
        # Calculate distribution frequencies
        freq = [0] * n
        # Distribution goes to boxes: (start+1) % n, (start+2) % n, ..., (start+k) % n
        for j in range(1, k + 1):
            box = (start + j) % n
            freq[box] += 1
        
        # Check if all conditions are satisfied
        valid = True
        for j in range(n):
            if j == start:
                if final[j] + k - freq[j] < 0:
                    valid = False
                    break
            else:
                if final[j] - freq[j] < 0:
                    valid = False
                    break
        
        if valid:
            # Found a valid solution
            original = [0] * n
            for j in range(n):
                if j == start:
                    original[j] = final[j] + k - freq[j]
                else:
                    original[j] = final[j] - freq[j]
            print(' '.join(map(str, original)))
            break
            
        k += n
```

```python
n, x = map(int, input().split())
final = list(map(int, input().split()))

# Try each possible starting box (0-indexed)
for start in range(n):
    target = x - 1  # 0-indexed target
    
    # k must satisfy (start + k) % n = target
    k0 = (target - start) % n
    if k0 == 0:
        k0 = n
    
    k = k0
    while True:
        # Calculate distribution frequencies
        freq = [0] * n
        # Distribution goes to boxes: (start+1) % n, (start+2) % n, ..., (start+k) % n
        for j in range(1, k + 1):
            box = (start + j) % n
            freq[box] += 1
        
        # Check if all conditions are satisfied
        valid = True
        for j in range(n):
            if j == start:
                if final[j] + k - freq[j] < 0:
                    valid = False
                    break
            else:
                if final[j] - freq[j] < 0:
                    valid = False
                    break
        
        if valid:
            # Found a valid solution
            original = [0] * n
            for j in range(n):
                if j == start:
                    original[j] = final[j] + k - freq[j]
                else:
                    original[j] = final[j] - freq[j]
            print(' '.join(map(str, original)))
            break
            
        k += n
```

Submitted Code

View Extracted Code 
n, x = map(int, input().split())
final = list(map(int, input().split()))

# Try each possible starting box (0-indexed)
for start in range(n):
    target = x - 1  # 0-indexed target
    
    # k must satisfy (start + k) % n = target
    k0 = (target - start) % n
    if k0 == 0:
        k0 = n
    
    k = k0
    while True:
        # Calculate distribution frequencies
        freq = [0] * n
        # Distribution goes to boxes: (start+1) % n, (start+2) % n, ..., (start+k) % n
        for j in range(1, k + 1):
            box = (start + j) % n
            freq[box] += 1
        
        # Check if all conditions are satisfied
        valid = True
        for j in range(n):
            if j == start:
                if final[j] + k - freq[j] < 0:
                    valid = False
                    break
            else:
                if final[j] - freq[j] < 0:
                    valid = False
                    break
        
        if valid:
            # Found a valid solution
            original = [0] * n
            for j in range(n):
                if j == start:
                    original[j] = final[j] + k - freq[j]
                else:
                    original[j] = final[j] - freq[j]
            print(' '.join(map(str, original)))
            break
            
        k += n
